Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Wed Apr 16 2014 03:27:32 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

भाजपा, आआप तथा कांग्रेस के चुनाव घोषणापत्रों में समानताएं :

1. तीनों ही पार्टियां विदेशी कंपनियों को एफडीआई (FDI) के माध्यम से मिलनेवाली छूट का समर्थन करती हैं जिसके कारण भारत में विदेशी कंपनियों को आय कर तथा कई अन्य कर नही चुकाने पड़ते। तथा ये तीनों पार्टियां इस बुरे कानून को बरक़रार रखने पर जोर देती हैं।

2. काशी विश्वनाथ देवालय तथा कृष्ण जन्मभूमि देवालय के मुद्दे पर तीनों हीं घोषणापत्र मौन हैं।

3. तीनो पार्टियां बातें तो बड़ी बड़ी करती हैं लेकिन उन्हें लागू करने के लिए कोई कानून ड्राफ्ट नही देतीं। आआप ने लोकपाल ड्राफ्ट प्रस्तावित किया, जो कि दंडात्मक शक्तियों के केंद्रीकरण का बदतर रूप प्रस्तुत करता है , तथा यह एक ऐसा ड्राफ्ट है जो भारत को चांदी प्लेट में रखकर बड़ी आसानी से विदेशी ताकतों के हाथों में सौंप देगा। तथा किसी अन्य मुद्दे पर आआप ने कोई ड्राफ्ट नही दिया। भाजपा तथा कांग्रेस के पास भी कोई ड्राफ्ट नही है।

4. तीनो घोषणापत्र बांग्लादेशियों को देश से निकालने का प्रश्न टाल जाते हैं।

5. ये सभी घोषणापत्र जनसँख्या नियंत्रण तथा उभरते हुए लोकतान्त्रिक असमानताओं पर भी चुप हैं।

6. ये घोषणापत्र कहते हैं "कर नियमों का सरलीकरण होना चाहिए", किन्तु इसे विस्तार से स्पष्ट नहीं करते।

7. तीनो घोषणापत्र उच्चतम तथा उच्च न्यायालयों के न्यायाधीशों, निचली अदालतों के न्यायाधीशों, पुलिस आदि में भाई भतीजावाद तथा भ्रष्टाचार की द्रुतगामी वृद्धि को कम करने के बारे में भी चुप हैं।

8. तीनो ही घोषणापत्र महिलाओं पर हो रहे अत्याचारों को अनदेखा कर रहे हैं, चूँकि बाल अपराध कानून (जुवेनाइल लॉ) के अनुसार 18 वर्ष से कम उम्र में अपराध करने पर 3 साल से ज्यादा की सजा नही मिल सकती, चाहे कितनी भी बार अपराध किया हो अथवा अपराध कितना भी गंभीर प्रकृति का हो। संयोग से यह कानून 2001 ई. में भाजपा ने ही बनाया था, किन्तु कांग्रेस तथा आआप ने भी कभी इसका विरोध नही किया।

9. हालांकि इनमें कुछ अंतर भी है, किन्तु तीनो की बाते समान रूप से चिंताजनक हैं। उदाहरण के लिए भाजपा का घोषणापत्र रक्षा क्षेत्र में FDI का समर्थन करता है, जिससे कि भारतीय हथियार व्यवसाय विदेशी कंपनियों की दया पर निर्भर हो जायेगा। वहीँ कांग्रेस तथा आआप के पास हथियार निर्माण को बढ़ावा देने के लिए कोई प्रस्ताव ही नही है।
इन तीनों पार्टियों ने अपना चुनाव घोषणापत्र बिलकुल आखिर में दिया, नामांकन की प्रक्रिया शुरू होने के कुछ ही दिनों पहले।

घोषणापत्र हमेशा से बेकार तो माने ही जाते रहे हैं, किन्तु इस चुनाव ने तो घोषणापत्रों की अनुपयोगिता की सारी हदें ही पार कर लीं।

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Wed Apr 16 2014 03:27:32 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

भाजपा, आआप तथा कांग्रेस के चुनाव घोषणापत्रों में समानताएं :

1. तीनों ही पार्टियां विदेशी कंपनियों को एफडीआई (FDI) के माध्यम से मिलनेवाली छूट का समर्थन करती हैं जिसके कारण भारत में विदेशी कंपनियों को आय कर तथा कई अन्य कर नही चुकाने पड़ते। तथा ये तीनों पार्टियां इस बुरे कानून को बरक़रार रखने पर जोर देती हैं।

2. काशी विश्वनाथ देवालय तथा कृष्ण जन्मभूमि देवालय के मुद्दे पर तीनों हीं घोषणापत्र मौन हैं।

3. तीनो पार्टियां बातें तो बड़ी बड़ी करती हैं लेकिन उन्हें लागू करने के लिए कोई कानून ड्राफ्ट नही देतीं। आआप ने लोकपाल ड्राफ्ट प्रस्तावित किया, जो कि दंडात्मक शक्तियों के केंद्रीकरण का बदतर रूप प्रस्तुत करता है , तथा यह एक ऐसा ड्राफ्ट है जो भारत को चांदी प्लेट में रखकर बड़ी आसानी से विदेशी ताकतों के हाथों में सौंप देगा। तथा किसी अन्य मुद्दे पर आआप ने कोई ड्राफ्ट नही दिया। भाजपा तथा कांग्रेस के पास भी कोई ड्राफ्ट नही है।

4. तीनो घोषणापत्र बांग्लादेशियों को देश से निकालने का प्रश्न टाल जाते हैं।

5. ये सभी घोषणापत्र जनसँख्या नियंत्रण तथा उभरते हुए लोकतान्त्रिक असमानताओं पर भी चुप हैं।

6. ये घोषणापत्र कहते हैं "कर नियमों का सरलीकरण होना चाहिए", किन्तु इसे विस्तार से स्पष्ट नहीं करते।

7. तीनो घोषणापत्र उच्चतम तथा उच्च न्यायालयों के न्यायाधीशों, निचली अदालतों के न्यायाधीशों, पुलिस आदि में भाई भतीजावाद तथा भ्रष्टाचार की द्रुतगामी वृद्धि को कम करने के बारे में भी चुप हैं।

8. तीनो ही घोषणापत्र महिलाओं पर हो रहे अत्याचारों को अनदेखा कर रहे हैं, चूँकि बाल अपराध कानून (जुवेनाइल लॉ) के अनुसार 18 वर्ष से कम उम्र में अपराध करने पर 3 साल से ज्यादा की सजा नही मिल सकती, चाहे कितनी भी बार अपराध किया हो अथवा अपराध कितना भी गंभीर प्रकृति का हो। संयोग से यह कानून 2001 ई. में भाजपा ने ही बनाया था, किन्तु कांग्रेस तथा आआप ने भी कभी इसका विरोध नही किया।

9. हालांकि इनमें कुछ अंतर भी है, किन्तु तीनो की बाते समान रूप से चिंताजनक हैं। उदाहरण के लिए भाजपा का घोषणापत्र रक्षा क्षेत्र में FDI का समर्थन करता है, जिससे कि भारतीय हथियार व्यवसाय विदेशी कंपनियों की दया पर निर्भर हो जायेगा। वहीँ कांग्रेस तथा आआप के पास हथियार निर्माण को बढ़ावा देने के लिए कोई प्रस्ताव ही नही है।
इन तीनों पार्टियों ने अपना चुनाव घोषणापत्र बिलकुल आखिर में दिया, नामांकन की प्रक्रिया शुरू होने के कुछ ही दिनों पहले।

घोषणापत्र हमेशा से बेकार तो माने ही जाते रहे हैं, किन्तु इस चुनाव ने तो घोषणापत्रों की अनुपयोगिता की सारी हदें ही पार कर लीं।

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Wed Apr 16 2014 03:28:43 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

प्रिय मोदी प्रेमियों, यहाँ मैं सचमुच एक प्रस्ताव रख रहा हूँ जो कि आपको वाराणसी के चुनावों के लिए उपयोगी लग सकता है।

सबसे पहले मैं यह स्पष्ट कर दूँ कि मैं मोदी भक्त नही हूँ। ऐसा मैं अवश्य मानता हूँ और कई बार कह भी चूका हूँ कि वे प्रधान मंत्री पद के लिए सारे उपलब्ध विकल्पों में सबसे अच्छे= कम बुरे व्यक्ति हैं। किन्तु एक अच्छा प्र. मं. भी अच्छे कानून ड्राफ्टों के अभाव में 1% काम भी नही कर सकता। अतः भारत को अभी सबसे अधिक जरुरत अच्छे कानून ड्राफ्टों की है।
अभी वाराणसी में सब कुछ इतना आसान नही है। AK-420 का समर्थन करने के लिए सऊदी ने मुख़्तार अंसारी को खरीदा है। अतः हो सकता है मुख़्तार अंसारी चुनाव में खड़े न हों। वाराणसी में 25% मुस्लिम जनसँख्या है। तथा सभी दलित तथा अन्य हिन्दू भी अनिवार्य रूप से भाजपा को ही वोट देंगे यह जरूरी नही। आखिरी क्षणों में कांग्रेस अपने दलित तथा स्लम के मतदाताओं से AK-420 को वोट देने के लिए कह सकती है।

AK-420 की लोकप्रियता मध्य वर्ग के मतदाताओं में हो गयी है। यदि AK-420 को मध्य वर्ग के मतदाता वोट दे देते हैं तो नमो के लिए मुश्किल कड़ी हो जाएगी। अब निर्णय आपको लेना होगा।

मैं तो निम्नलिखित तरीके से मदद कर सकता हूँ-

1. 30 अप्रैल के बाद, मैं वाराणसी में AK-420 का पर्दाफाश करने का काम कर सकता हूँ।

2. इसके लिए मैं कोई पैसा नही लूंगा।

3. आप मोदी भक्तों को ही मेरे आने- जाने, होटल आदि का खर्च वहन करना होगा। मैं बहुत खर्चीला नही हूँ, पर बहुत निम्नस्तर का भी नही हूँ। 2 स्टार एसी होटल से काम चल जायेगा, लेकिन इससे कम नही। साथ ही समय की कमी के चलते मैं ट्रेन में सफर नही कर सकता, अतः मैं फ्लाइट से ही आना जाना करूँगा। और मुझे बिजनेस क्लास की जरुरत नही है।

4. यह पैसा ऐसे व्यक्ति का होना चाहिए जो भाजपा केकिसी पद पर न हो। अतः वह व्यक्ति एक आम मतदाता होना चाहिए।

मैं ऐसा करने के लिए तैयार हूँ इसके पीछे कारण है ---- AK-420 ने कार्यकर्ताओं को गुमराह करके भारत की बहुत बड़ी क्षति की है। राइट टू रिकॉल आंदोलन को भी सर्वाधिक क्षति AK-420 ने ही RTR लाने का फर्जी वादा करके पहुँचाई है। नमो ने भी RTR का कभी समर्थन नही किया, किन्तु उन्होंने इसे कोई क्षति भी नही पहुँचाई। चूँकि उन्होंने पहले दिन से ही स्पष्ट रखा की वे व्यवस्था परिवर्तन, RTR आदि नही लायेंगे। जबकि AK ने बार- बार व्यवस्था परिवर्तन की बात कहकर कार्यकर्ताओं को गुमराह किया।

यदि आप चाहें तो मैं वाराणसी चुनाव इस प्रकार से लड़ूंगा कि एक भी वोट न कटे। लेकिन चूँकि मैं भी एक उम्मीदवार होऊंगा, अतः मैं उन्हें विरोधी उम्मीदवार के तौर पर वाद विवाद के लिए चुनौती दे सकता हूँ। एक बात और, जमानत की राशि भी आपको ही देनी होगी, साथ ही 10 समर्थकों के हस्ताक्षरों का इंतजाम भी करना होगा।
अतः कृपया सोचकर निर्णय कर लें।

AK-420 की लोकप्रियता मध्य वर्ग के मतदाताओं में हो गयी है। यदि AK-420 को मध्य वर्ग के मतदाता वोट दे देते हैं तो नमो के लिए मुश्किल कड़ी हो जाएगी। अब निर्णय आपको लेना होगा।

1. 30 अप्रैल के बाद, मैं वाराणसी में AK-420 का पर्दाफाश करने का काम कर सकता हूँ।

2. इसके लिए मैं कोई पैसा नही लूंगा।

3. आप मोदी भक्तों को ही मेरे आने- जाने, होटल आदि का खर्च वहन करना होगा। मैं बहुत खर्चीला नही हूँ, पर बहुत निम्नस्तर का भी नही हूँ। 2 स्टार एसी होटल से काम चल जायेगा, लेकिन इससे कम नही। साथ ही समय की कमी के चलते मैं ट्रेन में सफर नही कर सकता, अतः मैं फ्लाइट से ही आना जाना करूँगा। और मुझे बिजनेस क्लास की जरुरत नही है।

4. यह पैसा ऐसे व्यक्ति का होना चाहिए जो भाजपा केकिसी पद पर न हो। अतः वह व्यक्ति एक आम मतदाता होना चाहिए।

मैं ऐसा करने के लिए तैयार हूँ इसके पीछे कारण है ---- AK-420 ने कार्यकर्ताओं को गुमराह करके भारत की बहुत बड़ी क्षति की है। राइट टू रिकॉल आंदोलन को भी सर्वाधिक क्षति AK-420 ने ही RTR लाने का फर्जी वादा करके पहुँचाई है। नमो ने भी RTR का कभी समर्थन नही किया, किन्तु उन्होंने इसे कोई क्षति भी नही पहुँचाई। चूँकि उन्होंने पहले दिन से ही स्पष्ट रखा की वे व्यवस्था परिवर्तन, RTR आदि नही लायेंगे। जबकि AK ने बार- बार व्यवस्था परिवर्तन की बात कहकर कार्यकर्ताओं को गुमराह किया।

यदि आप चाहें तो मैं वाराणसी चुनाव इस प्रकार से लड़ूंगा कि एक भी वोट न कटे। लेकिन चूँकि मैं भी एक उम्मीदवार होऊंगा, अतः मैं उन्हें विरोधी उम्मीदवार के तौर पर वाद विवाद के लिए चुनौती दे सकता हूँ। एक बात और, जमानत की राशि भी आपको ही देनी होगी, साथ ही 10 समर्थकों के हस्ताक्षरों का इंतजाम भी करना होगा।
अतः कृपया सोचकर निर्णय कर लें।

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Wed Apr 16 2014 03:30:15 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

जनसंघ का मूल झंडा (पताका) पूर्णतः नारंगी (भगवा) रंग का था। साथ ही यह चौकोर न होकर तिकोना था। क्योकि सभी हिन्दू राजाओं, संतों आदि के झंडे तिकोने ही होते थे, तथा चौकोर झंडा प्रायः पश्चिम में उपयोग किया जाता था।

1980 में जब भाजपा की स्थापना हुई, इसका झंडा चौकोर होने के साथ- साथ कुछ भाग हरे रंग का भी था। तथा हरा रंग नारंगी के पहले था। कई आधिकारिक तस्वीरों में कमल का रंग भी नारंगी है, किन्तु इसकी निचली बाह्य पंखुरियों का रंग हरा होता है !! और यह हरा रंग एक मुख्य कारण है जिसके चलते बलराज मधोक जैसे पुराने समय के कई जनसंघियों ने भाजपा में शामिल होने से मना कर दिया।

सारांश यह है कि 1980 ई. में भाजपा ने केसरिया रंग को फीका किया, तथा नए घोषणापत्र में तो इससे नाता ही तोड़ लिया। यहाँ तक कि बिलकुल ज्वलंत समस्याओं, जैसे सभी धर्मों में बाल विवाह पर रोक लगाने, तीन तलाक पर रोक लगाने, तथा बहु विवाह रोकने हेतु कोई समयसीमा नही दी गयी है।

2012 के आरम्भ में ही भाजपा ने नया लोगो बनाया जिसमें शीर्ष भाग तो नारंगी था तथा निचला हिस्सा हरा था, किन्तु पूरे कमल का रंग सफ़ेद था !! सफ़ेद रंग क्रिश्चियन धर्म का प्रतीक है !!

आनेवाले कुछ साल हिंदूवादियों के लिए आघात एवं चिंता का विषय होंगे। तीन मंदिरों की तो बात ही भूल जाओ, यहाँ तक कि सभी धर्मों में बाल विवाह पर रोक, बहुविवाह, तीन तलाक, न्यायालय को गुजारा भत्ता तथा जनसँख्या नियंत्रण आदि पर निर्णय का अधिकार देने आदि जैसे ज्वलंत मुद्दे भी सत्ता की खिड़की से बाहर फेंक दिए जायेंगे।

इसके लिए समाधान मैं प्रस्तावित कर रहा हूँ कि हिंदूवादी (तथा स्वदेशी एवं राष्ट्रवादी) अपनी संस्था भक्ति जैसे आरएसएस/ भाजपा/ विहिप आदि की भक्ति छोड़ दे। तथा इसकी जगह वे कानून ड्राफ्टों पर ध्यान केंद्रित करें, जिससे सभी मुद्दे हल हो जायेंगे। मेरे द्वारा कई कानून ड्राफ्ट प्रस्तावित किये गए हैं। कृपया rahulmehta . com / 301.htm को डाउनलोड करें। मैंने इस सम्बन्ध में अत्यंत महत्वपूर्ण कानून ड्राफ्ट प्रस्तावित किया है जो कि मंदिरों की व्यवस्था सुधारने के उद्देश्य से बनाई गयी है। यह फेसबुक पर facebook. com / note.php ? note_id=10151972436171922 के नोट में आप पढ़ सकते हैं।

लेकिन यदि कार्यकर्ता संस्था भक्ति तक सीमित रहते हैं तो विनाश अवश्यम्भावी है।

एपल, माइक्रोसॉफ्ट, गूगल, फेसबुक आदि कम्पनियाँ सैंकड़ो अच्छे सॉफ्टवेयर बनाती हैं। इसका कारण यह नही है कि इनके पास अच्छे सीईओ हैं, बल्कि इनके पास हजारों अच्छे प्रशिक्षित इंजीनियर हैं जो कि अच्छे सॉटवेयर ऍप्लिकेशन लिख सकते हैं तथा दूसरी कंपनियों के अच्छे सॉफ्टवेयर का विश्लेषण भी कर सकते हैं। ठीक इसी प्रकार किसी देश के राजनीतिज्ञ अच्छे कानून ड्राफ्ट सिर्फ तभी बना सकते हैं यदि दसियों हजार कार्यकर्त्ता ऐसे हों जो अच्छे कानून ड्राफ्ट लिखने तथा उनके विश्लेषण की योग्यता रखते हैं। दुर्भाग्य से, कांग्रेस, भाजपा, आआपा आदि सभी पार्टियां अपने कार्यकर्ताओं से अपने दिमाग बंद रखने, कानून ड्राफ्ट नही पढ़ने तथा दूसरे देशों के कानून ड्राफ्टों का विश्लेषण नही करने को कहते हैं। तथा कांग्रेस, भाजपा, आआपा आदि सभी पार्टियां अपने कार्यकर्ताओं से कहते हैं कि वे नेता के चेहरे का टैटू बनवाएं, नारेबाजी करें, टोपी पहनें, मुखौटे पहनें, झाड़ू लगाएं, तथा कुछ और न करें। अतः इस प्रकार की राजनीति कभी भी हजारों प्रशिक्षित दिमाग नही बना सकती जिनकी आवश्यकता राष्ट्र की उन्नति हेतु अच्छे कानून ड्राफ्ट बनाने के लिए होती है।
कल्पना कीजिये क्या होगा यदि माइक्रोसॉफ्ट के सभी इंजीनियर सिर्फ नारेबाजी करें तथा यह साबित करने की कोशिश करें कि बिल गेट्स ही सबसे अच्छा सीईओ है, और अपने प्रतियोगियों के कोड और सॉफ्टवेयर के बारे में जानकारी न रखें। अब कल्पना करें कि एप्पल कंपनी के सभी इंजीनियर एप्पल कैप पहनें या स्टीव जॉब्स की मास्क पहनना शुरू कर दें तथा कोड लिखने पढ़ने में समय न दें, तो क्या होगा। ऐसे में वह कंपनी एक भी अच्छा प्रोडक्ट नही बना सकती।

1998 से लेकर अब तक, मैं भारत में एकमात्र ऐसा सीनियर कार्यकर्ता रहा हूँ जिसने हमेशा अपने जूनियरों को प्रशिक्षित किया है कि वे शत्रु राष्ट्रों के ऐसे कानूनों को पढ़ें जिनके चलते ये राष्ट्र सशक्त बने, तथा ऐसे ड्राफ्टों को पढ़ें, समझें तथा विश्लेषण करें जो कि भारत में भी समान प्रकार की कानूनी व्यवस्था ला सकते हैं। अतः आज लगभग 100- 300 कार्यकर्ता ऐसे हैं जो कि राइट टू रिकॉल ड्राफ्ट, ज्यूरी सिस्टम ड्राफ्ट, संपत्ति कर ड्राफ्ट आदि के बारे में जानते हैं। अन्य सभी नेता अपने कार्यकर्ताओं से मात्र नारेबाजी आदि तक सीमित रहने को कहते हैं।

अतः संक्षेप में इसे इस प्रकार समझ सकते हैं कि भारतीय राजनीति एक कंपनी की तरह है, जहाँ कि अधिकांश कार्यकर्ता = इंजीनियर सिर्फ नारेबाजी, कैप लगाना, मास्क पहनना, झाड़ू लगाना, अपने नेता के चेहरे का टैटू लगाना आदि ही जानते हैं। अतः भविष्य अच्छा नही दिखता।

इसके लिए मैं यह समाधान प्रस्तावित करता हूँ कि कार्यकर्ता इस प्रकार की दिमाग बंद करने वाली गतिविधियों जैसे झाड़ू लगाना, मास्क पहनना, नारेबाजी, टोपी पहनना, आदि को बंद करें तथा समस्याओं का समाधान करने वाले ड्राफ्टों पर ध्यान केंद्रित करें। तथा मतदाताओं से भी अनुरोध करें कि सांसदों को URL के साथ एसएमएस द्वारा इन ड्राफ्टों के सम्बन्ध में आवश्यक आदेश भेजें। वरना हमारी राजनीति उस कंपनी की तरह हो जाएगी जहाँ इंजीनियर सिर्फ नारेबाजी जानते हैं और किसी कोड को लिखना न जानते हों।

एपल, माइक्रोसॉफ्ट, गूगल, फेसबुक आदि कम्पनियाँ सैंकड़ो अच्छे सॉफ्टवेयर बनाती हैं। इसका कारण यह नही है कि इनके पास अच्छे सीईओ हैं, बल्कि इनके पास हजारों अच्छे प्रशिक्षित इंजीनियर हैं जो कि अच्छे सॉटवेयर ऍप्लिकेशन लिख सकते हैं तथा दूसरी कंपनियों के अच्छे सॉफ्टवेयर का विश्लेषण भी कर सकते हैं। ठीक इसी प्रकार किसी देश के राजनीतिज्ञ अच्छे कानून ड्राफ्ट सिर्फ तभी बना सकते हैं यदि दसियों हजार कार्यकर्त्ता ऐसे हों जो अच्छे कानून ड्राफ्ट लिखने तथा उनके विश्लेषण की योग्यता रखते हैं। दुर्भाग्य से, कांग्रेस, भाजपा, आआपा आदि सभी पार्टियां अपने कार्यकर्ताओं से अपने दिमाग बंद रखने, कानून ड्राफ्ट नही पढ़ने तथा दूसरे देशों के कानून ड्राफ्टों का विश्लेषण नही करने को कहते हैं। तथा कांग्रेस, भाजपा, आआपा आदि सभी पार्टियां अपने कार्यकर्ताओं से कहते हैं कि वे नेता के चेहरे का टैटू बनवाएं, नारेबाजी करें, टोपी पहनें, मुखौटे पहनें, झाड़ू लगाएं, तथा कुछ और न करें। अतः इस प्रकार की राजनीति कभी भी हजारों प्रशिक्षित दिमाग नही बना सकती जिनकी आवश्यकता राष्ट्र की उन्नति हेतु अच्छे कानून ड्राफ्ट बनाने के लिए होती है।
कल्पना कीजिये क्या होगा यदि माइक्रोसॉफ्ट के सभी इंजीनियर सिर्फ नारेबाजी करें तथा यह साबित करने की कोशिश करें कि बिल गेट्स ही सबसे अच्छा सीईओ है, और अपने प्रतियोगियों के कोड और सॉफ्टवेयर के बारे में जानकारी न रखें। अब कल्पना करें कि एप्पल कंपनी के सभी इंजीनियर एप्पल कैप पहनें या स्टीव जॉब्स की मास्क पहनना शुरू कर दें तथा कोड लिखने पढ़ने में समय न दें, तो क्या होगा। ऐसे में वह कंपनी एक भी अच्छा प्रोडक्ट नही बना सकती।

1998 से लेकर अब तक, मैं भारत में एकमात्र ऐसा सीनियर कार्यकर्ता रहा हूँ जिसने हमेशा अपने जूनियरों को प्रशिक्षित किया है कि वे शत्रु राष्ट्रों के ऐसे कानूनों को पढ़ें जिनके चलते ये राष्ट्र सशक्त बने, तथा ऐसे ड्राफ्टों को पढ़ें, समझें तथा विश्लेषण करें जो कि भारत में भी समान प्रकार की कानूनी व्यवस्था ला सकते हैं। अतः आज लगभग 100- 300 कार्यकर्ता ऐसे हैं जो कि राइट टू रिकॉल ड्राफ्ट, ज्यूरी सिस्टम ड्राफ्ट, संपत्ति कर ड्राफ्ट आदि के बारे में जानते हैं। अन्य सभी नेता अपने कार्यकर्ताओं से मात्र नारेबाजी आदि तक सीमित रहने को कहते हैं।

अतः संक्षेप में इसे इस प्रकार समझ सकते हैं कि भारतीय राजनीति एक कंपनी की तरह है, जहाँ कि अधिकांश कार्यकर्ता = इंजीनियर सिर्फ नारेबाजी, कैप लगाना, मास्क पहनना, झाड़ू लगाना, अपने नेता के चेहरे का टैटू लगाना आदि ही जानते हैं। अतः भविष्य अच्छा नही दिखता।

इसके लिए मैं यह समाधान प्रस्तावित करता हूँ कि कार्यकर्ता इस प्रकार की दिमाग बंद करने वाली गतिविधियों जैसे झाड़ू लगाना, मास्क पहनना, नारेबाजी, टोपी पहनना, आदि को बंद करें तथा समस्याओं का समाधान करने वाले ड्राफ्टों पर ध्यान केंद्रित करें। तथा मतदाताओं से भी अनुरोध करें कि सांसदों को URL के साथ एसएमएस द्वारा इन ड्राफ्टों के सम्बन्ध में आवश्यक आदेश भेजें। वरना हमारी राजनीति उस कंपनी की तरह हो जाएगी जहाँ इंजीनियर सिर्फ नारेबाजी जानते हैं और किसी कोड को लिखना न जानते हों।

Wed Apr 16 2014 12:43:28 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Anatomy of my anti-BJP,. anti-AAP , anti-Congress statements

For a past several years, I have been opposing the FACT that as per various tax laws, the foreign companies pay zero income etc taxes while Indians have to pay taxes. Now did any Congress, BJP, AAP leaders tell you about this FACT in our tax laws? NO. They never did. Do you want that I should also hide this tax law fact from activists and voters?

And for several years, I have been telling all activists that that each every Congress-leader, each and every BJP-leader and each and every AAP leader has been supporting this nefarious tax law. Should I hide this fact from all? Should I tell lies to all that "Congress, BJP, AAP leaders are opposing this law"? Or do you want me to push this fact about leaders under the carpet.

Likewise, I have been telling all since jan-2012 than Govt of India has hidden hindu % population in census-2011 numbers, to hide growing demographic imbalance. And Govt of India has also hidden % of bengali speaking population in India to hide Bangladeshi infiltration problem. Do YOU want to cover up this fact?

And since jan-2012, I have also been telling all that BJP-leaders and AAP-leaders have NOT demanded these census-2011 reilgion/language numbers. Should I hide these facts as well?

And what is YOUR opinion about this tax law, that "gora pays no tax, Indians have to pay taxes"? Do you think that this legal fact should be hidden from voters? And do you also insisting on hiding the fact that BJP\Congress\AAP leaders hide these facts? And what is YOUR opinion on Govt's hiding census-2011 religion/language data? And what is your opinion on BJP and AAP not demanding religion/language data? Can YOU post YOUR opinion on these issues on YOUR walls and your friends' walls?

And likewise, I noticed that flag of Jansagh was saffron, that of BJP in 1980 was 33% green. And lotus in 1980 was all 100% saffron, but in 1990, lower petals of that BJP's lotus became green and now BJP's lotus is 100% white !!! Do you insist that I should hide this FACT too?

IOW, can you tell me a sentence or para in my posts which is lie? And something that I should hide from activists and not tell them?

At the end of the day, I consider "chugalkhori" as my dharam.. I spend time in making proposals such as RTR-drafts. drafts to strengthen Hindu sects, drafts to bring population control, MRCM-draft to reduce poverty , drafts to reduce high interest rates on poor etc etc. And I also spend time to dig FACTS and spread all IMPORTANT FACTS to all. Now if some FACTS go break image of YOUR pet party, be Congress or AAP or BJP, then please note that it is their fault ONLY. Please ask YOUR leaders via SMS to change their behavior so that damage to India reduces.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sat Apr 19 2014 02:10:01 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

BJP-leaders are spreading a lie that they changed lotus color from orange to white because EVM uses black and white papers !! And why am I saying this?

The paper in booth had always been black and white since 1920s when elections first started in India. That didnt stop ANY party from putting colors on their symbols in pamphlets. It is proven that people while recognizing objects look as shape and boundaries and ignore colors !! Forget simple handbills and posters, BJP-leaders now insist on white lotus even on BJP website !! I am sure that people who come to websites are smart enough to see that orange lotus is same as white lotus. So why change color of lotus on website? Also, BJP is national party, and so its candidate will be in first band, and so almost always in one of the top 5 slots. So it would NOT be difficult for voter to find black and white lotus, even if lotus on flag etc is orange.

Please see Modifesto of dec-2012. The lotus is saffron. Come jan-2014, the color of lotus changes to white on all official sources including Modifesto of apr-2014 !!!

The MNC-owners and Missionaries now run charities , self-help groups, women credit unions , NGOs etc which span over 7% to 10% population DIRECTLY. And situation in education is worse. The RSS-apex asked RSS-workers to confine to slogan shouting and morning walks , and oppose all law-drafts needed to improve education. And education across India in govt schools deteriorated. And so the missionarty schools are now only schools in many tribal/dalit areas of India including Gujarat !!! In Gujarat, the Municipals schools have rotted to an extent that no municipal school passout passes 12th class exam !! And so now poor increasingly send kids to missionary run schools, even in Ahmedabad. And so church has huge vote base, thanks ONLY to RSS-apex's decision to oppose law-drafts needed to improve education. So BJP-leaders have to depend on missionaries for votes. And BJP-leaders now heavily depend on MNC-owned media. This is the ONLY reason why BJP-leaders changed the color of lotus from orange to white, NOT some ballot paper reason.

There is more to it. Please google on "bjp manifesto 2012 gujarat images" and see 2012 Gujarat BJP manifesto. The kurta was orange and the jacket on top of kurta would be dark brown. OR, kurta would be white and jacket on top will be orange. And lotus was orange. But in recent interview on TV, NaMo was wearing a green kurta, and lotus was white.

The BJP-leaders have been known for color changes since 1980. The Jansangh flag was 100% orange. In 1980, BJP flag became 33% green. And in year 2002, the outer lower petals of the lotus changed from orange to green. The reason given was that lower petals are sepals. Thats nonsense. The sepals go upwards and not downwards. Also, when lotus blossoms, the outer rim of petals bend downwards and cover the sepals. eg please see any image of Godess Laxmi. You wont see any green sepals.

And now lotus has become white. And reason is not ballot paper.

And consider other decisions. BJP-leaders including NaMo refuses to demand census-2011 religion/language breakup in public. NaMo also now supports "gora shall pay no taxes, desi shall pay full taxes" regime that was started by IMF-agents RaoMMS in 1990s. And then legendary Sb4D remark, and then removal of Kashi Vishvanath Devalaya and Krishna Janambhoomi Devalaya issue from Modifesto. And then came decision to put Ram Janambhoomi Devalaya issue on the LAST page of Modifesto-2014 !!! All these decisions and now change of lotus color seen in one context imply that control of MNC-owners and Missionaries on BJP is rising day by day. And IMO, Hinduvaadies, Swadeshis and Nationalists should start looking for alternative as soon as possible.

WHY AM I WRITING THIS NOW?

I have been saying all this for years !!! Please see my wall --- it has been full of posts showing rising MNC-owners' control over BJP. And I have habit of telling FACTS I see to everyone ASAP. Many say that I shoudl wait till may-16. Well, what if I die between now and 16-may? Anyway, I have this superstition that spreading truth can do no harm to India. What I saw, I tell all, and thats how I wish to stay.

Existing tax laws say "Gora shall pay no tax, Indians must pay all taxes". One reason why such laws exist is because RSS workers, Bharat Swabhiman Trust workers etc insist on slogan shouting, and notgathering passing information on law-drafts !!

Since 2009, I have been asking Bharat Swabhiman Trust workers as well as RSS-workers to inform all citizens of India that

(1) existing tax laws impose near zero tax on foreign companies while while Indian companies have to pay all the taxes. With such tax laws, no Indian company will survive.

(2) BJP-leaders, Congress-leaders and now AK-420 have supported these tax laws.

But RSS-workers and BST-workers said "we want to confine to slogan shouting, member making, mask wearing etc etc, and hell with informing voters about these law-drafts and solutions !!!"

Net effect is ---- with all likelihood, these tax law is going to stay for next 5 years.

Solution is not easy. It is hard to convince slogan shouters to read , understand and act for changing tax laws or any laws. And as long as activists insist on ignoring law-drafts and confine to slogan shouting, I see no way to get rid of such bad tax laws

(1) existing tax laws impose near zero tax on foreign companies while while Indian companies have to pay all the taxes. With such tax laws, no Indian company will survive.

(2) BJP-leaders, Congress-leaders and now AK-420 have supported these tax laws.

But RSS-workers and BST-workers said "we want to confine to slogan shouting, member making, mask wearing etc etc, and hell with informing voters about these law-drafts and solutions !!!"

Net effect is ---- with all likelihood, these tax law is going to stay for next 5 years.

Solution is not easy. It is hard to convince slogan shouters to read , understand and act for changing tax laws or any laws. And as long as activists insist on ignoring law-drafts and confine to slogan shouting, I see no way to get rid of such bad tax laws

Read full post →

http://tinyurl.com/RtrDeo

The above three URLs point to same page.

If Govt Schools keep on rotting, Hinduism will die and so will whole India. And if any CM\PM tries to fix Govt schools, then owners of private schools will pay mediamen to ruin his image. Why? Because if Govt schools improve, then profits of private schools will fall. And so these owners of private schools will pay mediamen to ruin the image of leader. Thats why no CM\PM dares to or wastes his time in fixing Govt schools. IOW IMO the chair or PM or CM doesnt have strength to fix the Govt Schools. The solution I propose is Right to Recall District Education Officer. The details are given in chap-30 of http://rahulmehta.com/301.htm .

The solution I propose is ---- we should get Right to Recall law-draft printed in the Gazette. How? The method I propose is -- we activists should send the above 3 URLs as order to MLAs, and ask voters to the same. We should infor voters via newspaper ad etc.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Tue Apr 22 2014 06:41:17 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

1. In USA, if a student leaves college, he is NOT asked to pay fees of semesters he didnt attend. Where as in India , many colleges ask him to pay the fees of coming semester

2. Why Congress-apex, BJP-apex, AAP-apex , RSS-apex and VHP-apex etc oppose the proposals to improve Govt schools and make self finance colleges more competitive?

3. reason is : threat of school\college owners.

4. solution --- activists should send url to law-drafts to improve education as orders to MLAs via SMS.

=====

If Govt schools improve, then owners of private schools will lose income. And if private colleges are forced to become competitive, then also the owners of private colleges will earn less profits. So if any any leader tries to improve Govt schools or Govt colleges or tries to make private colleges competitive, then the owners will pay the paid-mediamen to ruin the image of that leader. Which is why no leader in India in any State is daring or wasting his time to improve Govt schools or Govt colleges or even try to make the private colleges competitive.

eg in USA, say I take admission in 8 semester course such as B Tech in any college. And say, I decide to change the college after 2-3 months. Then NO college in USA will dare to ask for fees for next 7 semesters !! Whereas in India, if a student wants to change college, then many colleges ask him to pay for the fee of rest of the semester. In fact , in USA, most colleges even refund a portion of fees of existing semester !!

Now State Govt or Central Govt can make a law in State\India that ---- if a college dares to ask for fee of the rest of the semesters , then the trustee will be thrown into prison for 4 years !! If such law is made, then no college will dare to ask for fees of rest of the semesters. And students will get option to change the college. This will force college owners to make colleges cheaper and\or improve quality. So we should print following law-draft in the Gazette.

======start of draft =====

1 If a student in any college wish to leave that college, then college shall NOT ask to pay fees for coming semesters. The students will have to only tghe fees\dues of current semesters and other unpaid dues of past semesters.

2. If college asks student ti pay fees of reminder of semesters, then the colleges trustees will be imprisoned for 4 years

====== end of draft ====

But no CM\PM will dare to print such law-draft in Gazette. Because if he does, the college owners via paid media will ruin that PM\CM's his career. The whats the solution

SOLUTION :

I have posted the above proposed law-draft at (aal three URLs point at same location)

http://activism.com/en_US/petition/law-to-punish-trustees-who-ask-for-fees-of-rest-of-the-semesters-if-the-student-wishes-to-leave-college/58869

http://tinyurl.com/RahulMehtaOrder5

http://tinyurl.com/CollegeChange

Please see these URLs, and if you like, then please send these three URLs to your MP\MLA via SMS as an order

  • http://activism.com/en_US/petition/law-to-punish-trustees-who-ask-for-fees-of-rest-of-the-semesters-if-the-student-wishes-to-leave-college/58869

As regards individual cases, any decision will depend upon the merits of the case as revealed by its details. "

RSS-apex and VHP-apex talk about protecting nation and Hinduvaad. These apex cant even protect the orange color in BJP lotus. So IMO , either of two protect anything. IMO both Mohan Bhagwat and Togadia are useless. And I request ALL RSS-activists and VHP-activists to dump them asap.

1. Jury System , Wealth tax to improve factories ; reduce corruption etc
,
2. RTR to further reduce corruption, increase growth

3. Inheritance tax, Wealth tax, cancel VAT, cancel service tax, cancel GST, cancel all tax benefits to FDI, cancel all tax benefits to SEZ, reduce income tax on all equally, cancel all tax benefits to all trusts including religious trusts including temples, gurudwara, mosques and churches

4. Law-drafts to strengthen each Hindu sects, law-drafts to reduce go hatya and many more

And we dont ask RSS-activists to "join" our organization. We only request them to expose their apex and tell TRUTH about their apex to all.

1. ABV in 1996 had made 3 promises "bhaya bhookh aur bhastachar door karenge" i.e. "we will remove hunger, fear and corruption". ABV in next 5-7 years attained which Congress could not in 50 years --- activists started accepting corruption as way of life and stopped bitching.

2. NaMo has convince all to dump Kashi Vishavanath Deavalaya, Krishna Janambhoomi,, accept FDI, accept "gora shall pay no tax, desi pays all tax" rules, accept service tax, vat , dilute RJB Devalaya --- something Congress could not in 60 years !! And that too before becoming PM !!!

Before 1996, every RSS-activist, BJP-activist and VHP-activist was anti-corruption. Congress could never convince them that "corruption should be accepted as way of life". Come next 5-7 years of ABV rule, and most RSS-activists etc started looking aside on corruption issue. ABV convinced them to "accept" corruption something which Congress couldnt in 50 years.

Theis was mainly because RSS-activists were given high hopes that ABV will remove corruption in few years. And ABV didnt even bother. Some Ministers were less corrupt than Congress , so corruption was less compared to Congress rule. But ABV did nothing to bring it down significantly.

And Congress has been trying to convince Hinduvaadies since 1988 that they should dump RJBD , KJBD, KVD etc issues. And since 1991 MMS and PVNR have been asking all to dump Swadeshi and accept "gora shall pay no taxes, desi pay all taxes" regime under the name of Mauritius treaty, SEZ etc etc. But Hindivaadies and Swadeshies never accepted.

But without become PM, NaMo has now convinced all Hinduvaadies and Swadieshies to dump both Hinduvaad and Swadeshi !!

Somehow, BJP is better at implementing Congress's agenda

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Wed Apr 23 2014 17:44:45 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

1. Democracy is only when activists and citizens impose the citizens' will on the leaders. The democracy is never due to so called "leaders with democratic mindset". It is pointless to say that such as such leader has undemocratic mindset. If undemocratic things are happening, then it is less due to "leader with undemocratic mindset" but more because the activists are not working to impose will of citizens on the leaders. And the nation needs a procedural setup to impose the will of citizens on leaders on a day to day basis on each and every issue. IMO, the activists should confine to creating such procedural setup, rather take will of citizens on issue by issue. Because activists are small in number and they dont have whole life for activism. Over 90% activists will spend no more than 5-12 hours a week, and that too for 4-6 years in their lives. So if the activists keep on taking issue by issue instead of creating procedural setup, then the activists will soon run out of time and never get anything done.

2. What is a good form of govt? What type of life should we live? How much industrialization is needed? How many hours in a week should we work? What should tax rate? And how much environment should be protected? Answers to all this depends heavily on what enemy is doing !!! If enemy has high tax rate and using that efficiently to build military, then we too need a matching military and for that we may need high tax . And if enemy is working 60 hours a week, and buidling powerful Military, and then we too may need to put similar hours. IOW, answers to many many questions depend on mainly what our enemy is doing !! We are NOT independent from enemies' decisions, even if we are not chained.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Fri Apr 25 2014 05:23:32 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

What do voters gain by supporting non-winners? Ans : 7300 voters supported me in apr-2011, and so today 1000s of volunteers across India know about Right to Recall, Jury System etc. , something which I could not do in election-less campaign of 1998-2009.

Every vote to a non-winning candidate takes the information on the law-drafts and problems he proposes to 10-20 volunteers.And that in turn forces other leaders to change stand. eg in apr-2009 some 7300 voters in Gandhinagar area supported me. And so 1000s of activists across India noticed the points I had mentioned. So the rate at which information was spreading increased. So much, that in april-2011, 100s of IAC workers told Anna that "Lokpal is bakwas, what if lokpal becomes corrupt? And Right to Recall is far better optoion !!" And this forced Anna to give lip service to Right to Recall. Otherwise, Anna\AK till apr-2011, never spoke anything in favor of RTR. Even today, both keep opposing every RTR-draft presented to them and never give their own RTR-drafts. This proves they both Anna\AK are anti-RTR. Then what forced them to give even lip service to RTR? The reason was their volunteers. And made their volunteers read information on RTR. It was fact that "7300 voters supported the RTR agenda in apr-2009".

To be specific

1. voter's support to me will inform more activists across India that voters should start sending necessary to orders to MPs via SMS containing URL to the law-draft that voters thinks is good for India.

2. voter's support to me will inform several activists about RTR, JurySys, wealth tax, putting all land ownership information of all trusts on net, imposing wealth tax on all trusts including temple, gurudwara, mosques, churches etc and so forth

3. voter's support to me will inform several activists about the fact that census-2011 relgion/language has been supressed by Govt and that every MP and every tall leader is silent about it

4. voter's support to me will inform several activists about Right to Recall District Education Officer, and how it can improve Municipal schools, and why it is must to improve Municipal School to save Hinduism, India, whatever

And many more.

One can say any good things about any party. But no party, be Congress or BJP or AAP ever publicized information on any good law-drafts. All they have done is wasting away time andd energy of activists in slogan shouting, cap wearing, mask wearing, jhadu wediling etc.

IOW, every voter's support to non-winner's agenda takes the information to 10-100 activists across India. Thats a major plus point and also it is only plus point AFAIS. And if winning is only reaon, why is BJP contesting polls in Tamil Nadu? It cant win even a single seat there. And throughout 1950s, Jan Sangh didnt win even municipal seat in large parts of India and Gujarat. But still it used to contest all elections. Contesting election is necessary to publicize the law-drafts one is proposing. Also, a non-winner is worth supporting only if he has law-draft proposals that would benefit India in long run. Supporting people like AK, whose only goal is oppose all good law-drafts like Right to Recall, Jury System, Wealth tax, repealing SEZ, repealing VAT, repealing service tax, repealing GST, etc. How only goal is cut votes of NaMo and nothing more. Voting for such sprurious newcomers surely serves no purpose.

Every vote to a non-winning candidate takes the information on the law-drafts and problems he proposes to 10-20 volunteers.And that in turn forces other leaders to change stand. eg in apr-2009 some 7300 voters in Gandhinagar area supported me. And so 1000s of activists across India noticed the points I had mentioned. So the rate at which information was spreading increased. So much, that in april-2011, 100s of IAC workers told Anna that "Lokpal is bakwas, what if lokpal becomes corrupt? And Right to Recall is far better optoion !!" And this forced Anna to give lip service to Right to Recall. Otherwise, Anna\AK till apr-2011, never spoke anything in favor of RTR. Even today, both keep opposing every RTR-draft presented to them and never give their own RTR-drafts. This proves they both Anna\AK are anti-RTR. Then what forced them to give even lip service to RTR? The reason was their volunteers. And made their volunteers read information on RTR. It was fact that "7300 voters supported the RTR agenda in apr-2009".

To be specific

1. voter's support to me will inform more activists across India that voters should start sending necessary to orders to MPs via SMS containing URL to the law-draft that voters thinks is good for India.

2. voter's support to me will inform several activists about RTR, JurySys, wealth tax, putting all land ownership information of all trusts on net, imposing wealth tax on all trusts including temple, gurudwara, mosques, churches etc and so forth

3. voter's support to me will inform several activists about the fact that census-2011 relgion/language has been supressed by Govt and that every MP and every tall leader is silent about it

4. voter's support to me will inform several activists about Right to Recall District Education Officer, and how it can improve Municipal schools, and why it is must to improve Municipal School to save Hinduism, India, whatever

And many more.

One can say any good things about any party. But no party, be Congress or BJP or AAP ever publicized information on any good law-drafts. All they have done is wasting away time andd energy of activists in slogan shouting, cap wearing, mask wearing, jhadu wediling etc.

IOW, every voter's support to non-winner's agenda takes the information to 10-100 activists across India. Thats a major plus point and also it is only plus point AFAIS. And if winning is only reaon, why is BJP contesting polls in Tamil Nadu? It cant win even a single seat there. And throughout 1950s, Jan Sangh didnt win even municipal seat in large parts of India and Gujarat. But still it used to contest all elections. Contesting election is necessary to publicize the law-drafts one is proposing. Also, a non-winner is worth supporting only if he has law-draft proposals that would benefit India in long run. Supporting people like AK, whose only goal is oppose all good law-drafts like Right to Recall, Jury System, Wealth tax, repealing SEZ, repealing VAT, repealing service tax, repealing GST, etc. How only goal is cut votes of NaMo and nothing more. Voting for such sprurious newcomers surely serves no purpose.

Sat Apr 26 2014 04:05:35 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

One of the MOST valid question get from many, including my 12th class old time friend is "what can independent at best do"? ---- Answer is : voters' support to an outside small candidate is the ONLY thing that can convince the good political party workers across parties to force their leaders to change stand !!!

The question of my class-12 friend is ---- (see link in comment) "But being an independent candidate If you win, do you think that you can bring out desired changes. I understand that BJP may not be an idealistic party on many counts. But as a matter of fact, to make any party ( to unite a people with altogether different mindset ) one has to sometimes compromise with their principle to some extent to achieve a greater cause.... Similarly it would be desirable if you can flex your thinking & join BJP & then convince them with your justification to achive your vision. Our country needs intellectuals like you. All the best dear."

Well, I will repeat what I said ---- that 7300 voters supported me in april-2009, and that convinced 100s of activists to read my Right to Recall, Jury System etc proposals, and impact was so high that Anna and AK in april-2011 were forced to give lip service to RTR.

Now if say 73000 voters support me this time, then 10000s of workers across BJP, Congress, AAP will come to know about Right to Recall will notice RTR , DDMRCAM (DDMRCAM = Direct Deposit of Mineral Royalties in Citizens Accounts and Military), JurySys etc law-drafts. And that will increase force on leaders to accept these law-drafts.

Please note the mechanism. The support of 7300 voters mean nothing to Anna, AK. But support of 7300 voters means a lot to activists !! And these activists influence Anna, AK a lot.

Same way 73000 voters support to me mean nothing to apex-leaders of India. But it would influence 10000s of activists across parties such as BJP, Congress, AAP etc.

If I join any party such as Congress, BJP or AAP , then the FIRST pre-condition will be that I will need to stop talking about DDMRCAM, RTR-drafts, Jury System etc. And thus activists will never get information about these proposed law-drafts.

And lastly, if I join Congress, BJP or AAP, then in future, no junior activists will trust any senior activist !! Just as AK's backstabbing (he first said he will never form party, and then he formed party) made many junior activist distrust all senior activists, my joining BJP , Congress, AAP will ruin the faith of juniors in rest of the mankind. Because I have been promising all that I will use all political support I get from them ONLY to publicize these drafts, and not to create a career in Congress, BJP or AAP. Now if I join Congress, BJP or AAP and make a career there, and they will never ever trust anyone again. So if I get tired, I better stay inactive for a while, take rest, come again or retire, but I should now never join Congress, BJP or AAP. Unless, these parties accept DDMRCAM-draft, RTR-draft and Jury System. And if that happens, we wont need Right to Recall Party, and we also wont need most of these parties !!!

So my focus is to get support of some 50000 voters --- that will be enough to motivate all good souls in all parties to read the proposed law-drafts. , then force their leaders and bring these laws in India

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sat Apr 26 2014 13:28:48 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

How tax exemptions in SEZ benefits videshi company more than desi companies

Congress\BJP\AAP have all pledged that they will continue tax benefits to SEZ companies. Let me explain how that benefits videshi far far more than desi. We are right to recall are now

Consider business of short export. Consider 10000 small manufacturers each having capacity of 10000 shirts a year on an average totaling 10 crore shirts per year. Now they may be exporting say 2 crore shirts a year.

Now consider one large videshi company which can also make 2 crore shorts a year in India and export the same.

Now the small desi companies dont have any marketing network in west. So even if they can export, they cannot give commitment of 100% export upfront !!! Whereas the videshi company can give commitment of 100% export upfront. So videshi comoany can setup SEZ, while these small players cant,

Now even if small players export, they will have to pay all taxes while company in sez wont have to pay tax. In addition to taxes, PF rules and labor laws will also apply on non-sez companies, while companies in sez will have no PF rules and they can hire-fire anytime !!! So due to tax differences, the desi company will simply lose all export business,

IOW, sez law favors companies which have sales and marketing network in USA, Europe etc. So it will mostly favor foreign companies and put Indian companies at a disadvantage.

Congress\BJ{\AAP support sez law-drafts. Bharat Swabhiman Trust workers insist on spending all time in slogan shouting and not waste time in opposing SEZ-law-draft !!! We at Right to Recall group are only people opposing tax breaks to sez comoanies !!!

In SEZ, Indians were able to establish software\IT companies . So now IT\software companies in sez are taxed at 20% !!! But most hardware, electronic , garment etc companies ra e wholly or partly videshi owned.And so they continue to get tax benefits,

===

In addition Congress\BJP\AAP also support Mauritus route which means if a foreign comoany opens an office in Mauritius, even if that office is just one room with one clerk who recieves posts, then that company will not be taxed on its profits etc. I asked many BST-workers to oppose Mauritius route, But here too, they want to confine to slogan shouting only.

If you are a voter in Gandhinagar Lokasabha constituency please consider
following plus points of candidate#16 , Rahul Chimanbhai Mehta . And if you not a voter there, then also please copy-paste this post, if you wish to spread the information

1. Rahul Mehta is the ONLY candidate who is informing lakhs of voters that Govt of India has suppressed the religion/language data of census-2011. and no BJP leader could find courage to ask for this data, or even inform voters that this data has been supressed !! Now more the votes he gets, more activists across Delhi, Mumbai, Kolkata, Gujarat, India etc will come to know that this data has been suppressed, And Rahul Mehta is the only candidate perhaps in whole of India, who is asking for public disclosure of this data

2. Rahul Mehta is started demand for Right to Recall in oct-1998, and demand caught speed after 7300 voters voted for him may-2009 election. Now if more voters vote for him, then the demand will further proceed. Please note that Anna\AK never spoke about RTR till apr-2011, and till date they have never given any DRAFT of Right to Recall, And both took out this Right to Reject to weaken and sidetrack the demand for Right to Recall. Rahul Mehta has been campaigning for RTR law over all positions such as PM, CM, MP, MLA, Supreme judge, High judge, District Police Chief, District Eduication Officer etc etc

3. Rahul Mehta is only candidate who is informing voters about JURY SYSTEM, and is also campaigning for law-draft to bring Jury System in India. All parties, be old or new, have opposed Jury System.

4. In addition, he has proposed and demand "Direct Deposit of Mineral Royalty into Citizens Account", since 1998. Again, every vote to him will make activists across Delhi, Mumbai, India etc notice this proposed law-draft. This law-draft can reduce poverty and this reduce conversions, improve education and also improve industries in India. '

5, In addition, he has been first and til date the only one to give a FULL law-draft by which citizens can "force" as well as enable District Police Chief to seekm prove and expel Bangladeshi infiltrators, And he proposed law-draft way back in 2004 to include all refugees from Bangladesh, Pakistan and Sri Lanka,

The whole issue -- some 7300 citizens supported him in may-2009, and effect was high enough to force Anna etc to say Right to Recall. So if he gets more support, then proposed law such as RTR, JurySys etc will get far more spread this time, and possibility of getting this law-drafts passed increased.

There are many additional proposed complete LAW-DRAFTS you can find on his manifesto at rahulmehta . com\301.htm , For more details, pls see his facebook profile "Rahul Chimanbhai Mehta".
